### DIM-10[MYS] STEP-004.007 COMMENT

### DIM-10[MYS] STEP-004.008 QUERY

### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.005 COMMENT

---
### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.006 EXEC

RESULT:
OK

### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.007 QUERY
SQL: select * from path_t order by val limit 3
RESULT:
ts|val|score|name
2024-01-01 00:00:00|1|1.5|alpha
2024-01-01 00:01:00|2|2.5|beta
2024-01-01 00:02:00|3|3.5|gamma
---
### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.008 COMMENT
-- Switch to local db
---
### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.009 EXEC
SQL: use fq02_switch_db
RESULT:
OK
---
### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.010 QUERY
SQL: select * from local_t limit 1
RESULT:
ts|val
2024-06-01 00:00:00|42
---
### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.011 COMMENT
-- [Rule 4] 1-seg ext table after USE local_db should fail (ext context cleared)
---
### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.012 QUERY
SQL: select * from path_t
RESULT:
ERROR: [0x2603]: Table does not exist
---
### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.013 COMMENT
-- 2-seg still reaches external from local context

### DIM-11[MYS] STEP-004.014 QUERY

2024-01-01 00:00:00|1|1.5|alpha

2024-01-01 00:02:00|3|3.5|gamma

RESULT:
